         function interval(func, wait, times){
            var interv = function(w, t){
               return function(){
                  if(typeof t === 'undefined' || t-- > 0){
                     setTimeout(interv, w);
                     try{
                        func.call(null);
                     }
                     catch(e){
                        t = 0;
                        throw e.toString();
                     }
                  }
               };
            }(wait, times);
            setTimeout(interv, wait);
         };

function addB(eid, bli){
if(typeof addBanner  == "function" ){

(function($) {

	"use strict";

	/**
	 *
	 * RoyalSlider active class module 
	 * @version 1.0.1:
	 *
	 * 1.0.1
	 * - Simplified code
	 * - Fixed bug when slide with activeClass is removed
	 */ 
	$.rsProto._initActiveClass = function() {
		var updClassTimeout,
			self = this,
			aSlideClass = 'rsActiveSlide';
		if(self.st.addActiveClass) {

			self.ev.on('rsOnUpdateNav', function() {

				if(updClassTimeout) clearTimeout(updClassTimeout);
				updClassTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
					if(self._oldHolder) self._oldHolder.removeClass(aSlideClass);
					if(self._currHolder) self._currHolder.addClass(aSlideClass);
					updClassTimeout = null;
				}, 50);
			});

		}
	};
	$.rsModules.activeClass = $.rsProto._initActiveClass;
})(jQuery);

function showSpec(datajson) {

jsn =datajson.spec;

      var random;
      var num = 0;
      lottery = {};
      var winner = '';
      var curr = pageInfo.cat.join(',');
      var show;
      var dentro = false;
      jQuery.each(jsn,function(key,val){


         show = true;
         var special = val.slug.split('|');
         var specialHome = val.slug_categoria_primaria;
         var current = curr.split(',');
         var found = jQuery.inArray(specialHome , current) > -1;
         if(found){
            show = false;
             dentro = true;
         }
         jQuery.each(current, function(el,v) {
            if (jQuery.inArray(v, special) > -1){
               show = false;
               dentro = true;
            }
         });
         var arr = special.concat(current);
         var sorted_arr = arr.sort();
         var results = [];
         for (var i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            if (sorted_arr[i + 1] == sorted_arr[i]) {
               results.push(sorted_arr[i]);
            }
         }
         var now = (new Date()).toISOString().substring(0, 10)
         if(val.datafine <= now){
            show = false;
         }

			var compete = true;
			if(val.promoInterna!=1){
				compete = false;
			}
			if(val.onlyforce==1 && compete){
				compete = false;
			}
			if(val.megaSpeciale!=1 && compete){
            compete = false;
         }
			if(val.sponsor!=1 && compete){
            compete = false;
         }

         if(compete && show){
            if(val.priority == 0){
               num = num+5;
            }else if (val.priority == 1){
               num = num+10;
            }
            lottery[num] = val.keyunique;
         }
         if(dentro) return false;
      })
		random=Math.floor(Math.random()*num)
      n = 0;
      jQuery.each(lottery,function(k,v){
         if(random >= n && random <= k){
            winner = v;
            return false;
         }else {
            n = parseInt(n)+parseInt(k);
         }
      });

      var sp = getObjects(jsn, 'keyunique', winner);
      if(jQuery.isEmptyObject(sp[0])){
         show = false;
      }
		if(getCookie('megaSpClose')== 'closed'){
			 show = false;
	   }

      if(show){
         if(sp[0].megaSpeciale == 1 && jQuery(".bigTail").length >0){

					}
            });
         }

      }



	function getCookie(cname) {
   var name = cname + '=';
   var decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
   var ca = decodedCookie.split(';');
   for(var i = 0; i <ca.length; i++) {
      var c = ca[i];
      while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
         c = c.substring(1);
      }
      if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
         return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
      }
   }
   return '';
};

   function getObjects(obj, key, val) {
      var objects = [];
      for (var i in obj) {
         if (!obj.hasOwnProperty(i)) continue;
         if (typeof obj[i] == 'object') {
            objects = objects.concat(getObjects(obj[i], key, val));
         } else if (i == key && obj[key] == val) {
            objects.push(obj);
         }
      }
      return objects;
   }
	 jQuery('body').on('click',' a.close',function(event){
      document.cookie="megaSpClose=closed; domain=."+location.hostname.split('.').reverse()[1]+"."+location.hostname.split('.').reverse()[0]+"; path=/";
      $(this).parent('.megaSp').hide();
   });

}

function loadInstagram() {  // called from componentWillMount()
    if (!window.instgrm) {
      const s = document.createElement('script');
      s.async = false;
      s.src = 'https://platform.instagram.com/en_US/embeds.js';
      s.id = 'react-instagram-embed-script';
      s.onload = this.onLoad;
      var bdy = document.body;
      if (bdy) {
        bdy.appendChild(s)
      }
    }
  }

function loadTwitter(){
	window.twttr = (function(d, s, id) {
	var js, fjs = d.getElementsByTagName(s)[0],
	t = window.twttr || {};
	if (d.getElementById(id)) return t;
	js = d.createElement(s);
	js.id = id;
	js.src = "https://platform.twitter.com/widgets.js";
	fjs.parentNode.insertBefore(js, fjs);
	t._e = [];
	t.ready = function(f) {
	t._e.push(f);
	};
	return t;
	}(document, "script", "twitter-wjs"));
}



function loadPin(){
pinJs = document.querySelectorAll('script[src*="assets.pinterest.com/js/pinit.js"]');
console.log('aaaa');
console.log(pinJs);
if(pinJs != null && pinJs.length){
window.document.removeChild(pinJs);
}
js = document.createElement('script');
js.src = '//assets.pinterest.com/js/pinit.js';
js.type = 'text/javascript';
document.body.appendChild(js);
}

		twttr.widgets.load();
	} else {
loadTwitter();		
	}
	if (typeof(FB) != 'undefined' && FB != null ) {
		FB.XFBML.parse();
	} else {
	   console.log('******* NO FB ----');
		(function(d,s,id){var js,fjs=d.getElementsByTagName(s)[0];if(d.getElementById(id))return;js=d.createElement(s);js.id=id;js.src='https://connect.facebook.net/it_IT/sdk.js#xfbml=1&version=v3.2';fjs.parentNode.insertBefore(js,fjs);}(document,'script','facebook-jssdk'));
	}
	if (typeof(window.instgrm) != 'undefined' && !window.instgrm) {
		window.instgrm.Embeds.process();
	} else {
		loadInstagram();
	}

function startMonitIS(el,id){
	console.log('IIS monitor '+id);
   monitoringIS[id] = VisSense(el).monitor();
   monitoringIS[id].on('visible', function(monitor) {
		insertArticle(id);
		monitoringIS[id].stop();
	});
   monitoringIS[id].start();
}

function insertAfter(el, referenceNode) {
    referenceNode.parentNode.insertBefore(el, referenceNode.nextSibling);
}

	//var thisElem = document.getElementById(id);
	var thisElem = document.getElementById(id);
	var link = infinityS[artIS].url;
	var content = infinityS[artIS].content;
	//content.replace('http://', 'https://');

		var copertina = '<div id="VEEDIO_hook_'+infinityS[artIS].video+'" class="trVEEDIO rsNoDrag vidContainer boxVideoPrimario" style="text-align:center;"><iframe id="veedio_iframe" frameborder="0" scrolling="no" width="600" height="338" src="https://player.veedio.it/video/'+infinityS[artIS].video+'/0/?1=1&width=600" allowfullscreen></iframe></div>';
	} else {

			 var copertina = '<div class="galleryCnt clearfix gallery-" id="ContaddFrame" style="margin: 25px 0 25px 0;"><div class="galleryImgCnt"><figure class="galleryImg fotoBtn"><a target="_blank" href="'+infinityS[artIS].album_link+'"><img id="fgcoverimg" alt="'+infinityS[artIS].album_title+'" src="'+infinityS[artIS].img+'"></a></figure></div></div>';

function monitorUrl(e,url,id){
	monitUrl[id] = VisSense(e).monitor();
   monitUrl[id].on('visible', function(monitor) {
		if(url != originUrl){
			if(history.pushState) {
				console.log("track ");

				if(urlcontata.indexOf(window.location.href)<= -1){
				urlcontata.push(window.location.href);
					console.log('DA TRACCIARE2');
				isssenetrack();
				} else {
					console.log('GIA TRACCIATA2');
				}
			}
		}
   });
   monitUrl[id].start();
}

function isssenetrack() {
	try {
		trEV++;
		_gaq.push(['_trackEvent', 'IScroll', 'pag_'+trEV]);
		_gaq.push(["_trackPageview"]);
		_gaq.push(["somma._trackPageview"]);
	} catch(err) {
		ga('send', 'event', 'Iscroll');
		ga("send", "pageview");
		ga("newTracker2.send", "pageview");
	}
	//	adRefresh();
	}
function adRefresh(){
	if(pageInfo.segment == 'desktop'){
		myrealoadAdv();
   } else {
		for(pos in pageInfo.ad){
			var skip = false;
			if(pos == 'slotconfig' && pageInfo.segment != 'desktop'){
				skip = true;
			}
			if(!skip){
				myrealoadAdv(pageInfo.ad[pos]);
			}
		}
	}
}

function randidis() {
  var text = "";
  var possible = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789";
  for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++)
    text += possible.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * possible.length));
  return text;
}
